Or I'll just skip eating the next day, or go run for hours. It's... it's been happening like this for a few months now.\n\nDoctor: Are you afraid of gaining weight?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I guess I am. It's kind of stupid, but... yeah. \n\nLike, even if my work pants are actually fitting looser, if I check the scale and it's up even just a single pound... I don't know. I just feel like I'm getting fat. And then I start worrying that I'm just gonna lose control of my whole body. So... yeah, I guess I do worry about it.\n\nDoctor: How often do you make yourself throw up after eating?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know exactly. Just... sometimes. If I eat way too much, I'll just go to the bathroom and do it. Or... or I'll take those laxatives. I just get scared of what all that food is gonna do to me, I guess. So I just have to get rid of it.\n\nDoctor: How many times a month would you say you have one of these episodes where you eat a lot more than you meant to?\n\nPatient: Um... like I said earlier, it's most weeks. So... maybe four or five times a month? \n\nAt least one night a week I'll eat like that, and then... you know, try to make up for it. By throwing up, or taking the laxatives, or just skipping meals or working out extra. It's just... it's been going on like that for several months now.\n\nDoctor: How many times a month do you make yourself throw up after eating?\n\nPatient: Uh... I mean, kind of like I just said. It's most weeks. \n\nOut of those four or five times a month, I guess I throw up for some of them. Other times I just use the laxatives, or... or work out extra hard, or skip eating, like I mentioned. It's just... whatever I do to make up for it. And yeah, it's been a few months of this.\n\nDoctor: How often do you take laxatives after eating?\n\nPatient: I... I don't really count them separate. Like I was just saying, it's... most weeks I have at least one night where I eat like that. And then I just have to try and make up for it somehow. \n\nSo it might be the laxatives, or throwing up, or... or skipping meals, or just working out extra. I just do one of them. I don't know exactly how many times for each specific thing. It's just... it's been going on like that for several months now.\n\nDoctor: Do you take laxatives after eating more often than you make yourself throw up?\n\nPatient: I don't know. Like I said, I don't really keep track of which one I do more. It just... depends on the day, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you use laxatives more often than you make yourself throw up after eating?\n\nPatient: I mean... you literally just asked me that. I really don't know. I don't keep track of which one I use more. It's just... whatever I end up doing.\n\nDoctor: Do you make yourself throw up after eating at least twice a week?\n\nPatient: I... look, I feel like we're just going in circles here. I already told you. Most weeks, there's at least one night where I eat a lot, and then I just... I do something to fix it. \n\nIt could be throwing up, or the laxatives, or just not eating the next day, or hitting the gym hard. I don't know if it's twice a week for throwing up specifically. I just know I've been doing... all of that... for a few months now.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. So... yeah, I guess I do worry about it.\n\nDoctor: How often do you make yourself throw up after eating?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know exactly. Just... sometimes. If I eat way too much, I'll just go to the bathroom and do it. Or... or I'll take those laxatives. I just get scared of what all that food is gonna do to me, I guess. So I just have to get rid of it.\n\nDoctor: How many times a month would you say you have one of these episodes where you eat a lot more than you meant to?\n\nPatient: Um... like I said earlier, it's most weeks. So... maybe four or five times a month? \n\nAt least one night a week I'll eat like that, and then... you know, try to make up for it. It's just... it's been going on like that for several months now.\n\nDoctor: How many times a month do you make yourself throw up after eating?\n\nPatient: Uh... I mean, kind of like I just said. Like I was just saying, it's... most weeks I have at least one night where I eat like that. I don't know exactly how many times for each specific thing. It's just... it's been going on like that for several months now.\n\nDoctor: Do you take laxatives after eating more often than you make yourself throw up?\n\nPatient: I don't know. Like I said, I don't really keep track of which one I do more. I really don't know. I don't keep track of which one I use more. It's just... whatever I end up doing.\n\nDoctor: Do you make yourself throw up after eating at least twice a week?\n\nPatient: I... look, I feel like we're just going in circles here.
